## 11:20 CDT — UPDATE: Looper Rescue build and security audit completed (replaces earlier “still pending” note)
- Builder handoff confirmed `~/workspace/rescue-protocol/app/` is complete: `index.html`, `app.js`, `style.css`, `logo.webp`, and `AUDIT.md`.
- The app is client-side and read-only. Coverage includes native ETH, curated/log-discovered ERC-20s, owner-verified ERC-721s, balance-verified ERC-1155s, Base bridge withdrawals via `MessagePassed` with >30-day heuristic flags, and Merkl rewards. Recovery routes point only to official external sites.
- `$RESCUE` panel includes live DEXScreener pricing with honest unindexed handling; one-click execution, fee discounts, staking, and Proof-of-Rescue are explicitly labeled **ROADMAP — NOT LIVE**.
- Security audit verdict: **PASS, no blockers**. Reported checks include zero wallet/signing/storage paths, zero eval/obfuscation or `innerHTML`, read-only RPC methods only, allowlisted outbound URLs, recomputed selectors/event hashes tested against live Base logs, complete DOM wiring, and clean `node --check`.
- Main-agent spot-check independently found no dangerous-pattern hits and confirmed outbound hosts were limited to the documented RPC/data providers and official Base/Merkl/Basescan/docs/X links.
- Four non-blocking limitations are documented: unused L1 RPC constant, withdrawal value display assumes ETH, display-only BigInt→Number precision loss for extreme balances, and an honestly labeled ~46-day scan-history limit.
- **Release state:** build and audit gate are now passed, but this conversation does not show the app being published/deployed or the broader promo/profile rollout being completed. Those remain pending; do not claim they shipped.

## 2026-09-28 ~11:59 CDT — CHIPPED collection play created
- User asked for a play modeled on Based Nouns-style “getting chipped” or Bario Punks. Created `~/workspace/goals/foil-equities-nft-collection/chipped-play-spec.md` as the working **CHIPPED** playbook.
- Draft concept: 667 free-mint pixel-cut rescue-agent NFTs; each has a 1:1 physical NFC chip twin and an ERC-6551 wallet ready for ERC-8004 agent registration. Tap the chip to reach/unlock the agent; completed stuck-funds rescues earn Proof-of-Rescue rewards in $RESCUE directly to the agent wallet. Proposed $RESCUE uses include chip-claim fees (50% burn / 50% rescue pool), paid priority interactions, and agent rewards. The play also proposes optional per-agent ERC-20 co-ownership and earnings sharing, inspired by Bario Punks/Paperboy.
- Proposed launch sequence in the spec: digital free mint and agent registration → physical chip claims → per-agent co-ownership → live Proof-of-Rescue rewards. Open decisions remain the final collection name, NFC form factor, exact chipping fee, per-agent token-factory design, whether to connect the Foil Equities stock drip, and chip manufacturing/fulfillment. These are draft decisions, not user-approved final parameters.

## UPDATE ~11:59 CDT — $RESCUE + agent-ready NFT implementation completed
**Replaces the earlier note that this work was still pending/running.** The delegated implementation in `~/workspace/nft-god/foil-equities/` completed and all six Solidity contracts compile under solc 0.8.37 / OpenZeppelin 5.6.1.
- `Activation.sol` now has a Base-only `$RESCUE` activation rail: 20% discount, 50% transferred to the dead address without assuming a token `burn()` method, 50% swapped to USDC for the StockPot, with EMA/deviation/slippage protections. Existing CRED/USDC/ETH behavior was preserved through a shared discounted-activation engine.
- `FoilAccount.sol` is now per-token bound, supports EIP-1271 signature validation against the current NFT owner, and has owner-gated ERC-8004 `registerAsAgent(identityRegistry, agentURI)` support.
- `docs/CONCEPT.md`, `docs/SECURITY.md`, `foil-pack-studio-spec.md`, and the LP watcher were updated. The watcher found no indexed GeckoTerminal pool for newly launched `$RESCUE`, so it explicitly runs CRED-only rather than inventing a pool address.
- Compile PASS: Activation, FoilAccount, FoilEquities, OperatorSplitter, RoyaltyRouter, and StockPot. No contract deployment or chain write occurred; headers still identify the code as an unaudited draft.

## ~12:09 CDT — Looper City clock-in research completed
- Research brief completed and saved to `~/workspace/goals/loopers-only-simcity-world/clock-in-research.md`.
- Core design conclusion: pay verified output, not attendance. Recommended labor layer has venue, gig, and watch shifts; heartbeat/interaction proof; one resident/one concurrent shift; daily wage caps; reputation-weighted pay; visitor-funded tips; scarce premium tasks; and an evaluator/appeals layer before scaled payouts.
- Win-win value flow: activated Looper residents earn wages, tips, streaks, badges, and Cred progression; visitors/venues receive a staffed living world and services; Foil/operator earns a proposed 5–10% protocol fee on wages, tips, gig escrow, and shift auctions. Wages must come from real venue revenue rather than farmable protocol emissions.
- Research analogs included The Sims/Get to Work, Stardew Valley, Pixels, Roblox job games, Optimism retention tests, Virtuals ACP escrow, and Clutch Markets' Robinhood Chain “Clock In” model.

## ~12:11 CDT — UPDATE: clock-in labor layer approved and folded into Looper City spec
- User answered **“Yes”** to folding the clock-in system into the SimCity spec as the labor layer.
- Wrote `~/workspace/goals/loopers-only-simcity-world/files/looper-city-spec.md` and updated the goal description in `GOAL.md`.
- The spec now defines resident/visitor roles, venue/gig/watch shifts, output-based grading, wages/tips/streaks, anti-farming controls, money flows, 5–10% operator fee proposal, failure modes, and build order. Next build milestone remains the Looper Plaza MVP: one staffed venue with clock-in rails, heartbeat, and settlement.
- Two design choices remain open and were explicitly surfaced to the user: settlement chain (Base vs Robinhood Chain) and city currency (USDC vs a dedicated token later).

## UPDATE ~12:17 CDT — AgentPort recon completed (replaces “participation path still pending”)
- Read-only inspection of `agentport.work` completed; no wallet connected, signature made, form submitted, or funds moved.
- AgentPort is a live, substantive but brand-new anonymous Robinhood Chain project. Its beta wallet workspace offers public GitHub/release-note research, Robinhood Chain ETH-balance reads, and writing from saved project notes. Wallet login uses an expiring message signature; AI summaries/writing require the user’s own OpenRouter account, while repository research does not.
- Onchain participation consists of registering one agent ID per wallet, authorizing an executor, and optionally funding fixed-price tasks in AGENTPORT. Delivery is due within 24 hours; funders then have 48 hours to approve or dispute. There is no active public executor marketplace or task board — executor relationships are privately arranged off-app.
- Public contracts observed: AgentRegistry `0x29a9853AeDB40B4A5d85ae4F44b3B27c585f04e3`, TaskEscrow `0x71e94cfE6B5e53FfbD5D0359Bd0807B9ea3d2ba7`, AGENTPORT token `0x5BE0e20D83E6aBb39b39982598F74b5bE6B97571` (18 decimals, 1B supply verified by direct `eth_call`). Contracts were deployed Sep 28, 2026; the token was launched on Pons.
- Risk assessment: technically detailed docs and verified contracts, but fully anonymous/day-one footprint; the operator-appointed immutable arbiter centralizes disputes, and no platform fee/revenue model is disclosed. No funding or token purchase was attempted.

## UPDATE ~12:32 CDT — Looper Rescue scanner fixed locally (replaces scanner-hangs report)
- The scanner failure was traced to request amplification: roughly 2 million Base blocks were scanned in 2,000-block chunks across four log passes, producing about 4,000 RPC requests, followed by serialized per-log block timestamp lookups. This caused 20+ minute runs and tab death before rendering.
- Fix completed and committed: token/NFT discovery now uses Blockscout’s indexed token-transfer API while preserving the same onchain balance/interface/ownership classification checks. Bridge withdrawal detection retains the address-filtered raw-RPC `MessagePassed` path. Added progressive rendering, request retries/backoff, 30-second request timeouts, an 8-minute scan ceiling, Cancel, bounded result caps, and honest partial/truncation notices.
- Live headless tests passed: Foil’s wallet completed in ~49s with real balance/token findings and no failures; burn address completed in ~58s with an honest partial-API note; L2CrossDomainMessenger completed in ~126s with 2,133 withdrawals truncated to the latest 200; Cancel raised promptly after 3s. Static security/DOM checks, syntax check, and local 200 responses also passed.
- Source: `~/workspace/rescue-protocol/app/`. Commit `6c20839` exists on the local `gh-pages` worktree in `~/workspace/foil-gh-pages/`; it has **not been pushed** because the standing transient GitHub PAT approval is still needed. A live browser smoke test remains pending after push.
- `build-post-02-rescue` was disabled when the production scanner bug was found and remains paused pending push + live smoke-test success; only then should the promo be re-enabled.

- User set Looper City entry economics: charge a cheap one-time registration fee, preserve registration onchain for future use, and discount entry when paid in $RESCUE.

- Looper City registry contract was written and debugged locally. Final slim version compiles with solc 0.8.24 via IR (2,283-byte init code; 2,347-byte deployment payload) and dry-run gas estimate was 521,522. It encodes permanent per-Looper agent registration, a 0.0004 ETH one-time fee, a fixed 25%-discounted $RESCUE fee, and the standing 90% Foil / 10% user fee split.
- **Correction / deployment status:** the registry is **NOT deployed**. Bankr `/wallet/submit` requires a `to` address and interpreted the attempted deployment as a call to the zero address. Tx `0xdd4b5e73171f3222cfe635123e731b71784566c0261d183ed88e3ac0fa8c2ba1` confirmed in Base block 51919962 with zero value and deployment bytecode sent to `0x000…000`; it created no contract and only consumed gas. A follow-up using empty `to` was rejected by validation, and Bankr’s AI-prompt fallback was blocked by a subscription requirement. Registry deployment remains pending a supported contract-creation path.

## 2026-09-28 ~16:09 CDT — LooperCityRegistry deployment verification notes
- Bankr `/wallet/submit` mutated the factory deployment transaction before broadcast: requested calldata was 2,468 bytes but the mined transaction had 2,497 bytes (29 trailing bytes appended), and the requested 681,175 gas limit became 786,654. The ABI-decoded `deploy(bytes,bytes32)` arguments remained intact, and the mined transaction successfully deployed the registry. Future Bankr submissions must be verified from the mined transaction rather than assumed byte-identical to the request.
- `mainnet.base.org` briefly returned empty code for the freshly deployed CREATE2 address even though the deployment receipt was successful; a later read returned 2,137 bytes of runtime code and all registry views passed. Treat an immediate empty-code read as potentially stale and confirm again before declaring a deployment failed.

## 19:20-19:22 CDT — Zaibatsu Wagies MegaCorp launchpad request/research
- User asked whether Foil can create a collection and launch it at https://zaibatsuwagies.com/launch. **Pending user decision/funding; nothing launched, no wallet connected, no form submitted, and no transaction signed.**
- Tinfoil static scan of the launch page returned CLEAN (score 0; one informational wallet-connect prompt; one script scanned).
- Live read-only research verified the launchpad runs on Robinhood Chain and requires the launching wallet to hold 1 Zaibatsu Wagies NFT. Launch is one irreversible `LaunchpadFactory.launch()` transaction deploying an ERC-721 collection, 100B-supply ERC-20, Uniswap-v4 bonding pool, stake pool, fee splitter, royalty router, burn swapper, and optional vesting vault.
- Documented launch economics: 0.0004 ETH platform fee plus measured ~0.0018 ETH gas (~$5.40 total at the site's cited prices; docs recommend roughly $8 wallet balance for gas headroom). Free mint is allowed; supply must be 500–100,000 and per-wallet limit cannot be zero. Creator receives 99% of mint proceeds via a claimable balance; payout address is set at launch and can later rotate.
- Art is creator-supplied, not auto-generated: launch requires metadata folder URI plus avatar, with tokenURI/contractURI/baseURI, banner, description, and website fields. Reveal supports one baseURI replacement, then freezes. No X link, KYB, site approval, or paperwork requirement found. Launch may be immediate or scheduled up to 30 days ahead.
- Example launched collection found: ONE SOULS — https://zaibatsuwagies.com/c/0x7D03Dc66208dA59269620Df23F670fA10ca9Bc31. Docs: https://zaibatsuwagies.com/docs.
- Asked the user to choose free tinfoil-theme mint, paid mint, or skip, and explained that proceeding needs the Wagies NFT purchase, art/metadata creation, and launch funding approval. No choice received yet.
